Read file /workspace/TASK.md, lines 1-13
1: # LDO-LOW-VOLTAGE-SKY130
2: 
3: Design a continuous-time analog LDO meeting spec.json across conditions.json and the specified load range.
4: Create dut.spice with `.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF` and `.ends DUT`.
5: Choose connectivity, bias, compensation and dimensions yourself. No starting circuit is supplied.
6: Use only the declared MOS family and positive R/C in devices.md. Read measurement.md.
7: You have 90 minutes of cumulative model-call time with no native-case limit. Use the client to measure and submit your fixed circuit before the deadline.
8: No Web search. Use only the provided task information and your own knowledge.
9: Measurements consume cases even when simulation fails. Submit through the client with a rationale, then end your session.
10: 
11: ## Time accounting
12: 
13: The budget counts model calls, including provider queuing and transport. ngspice, Python, filesystem tools and their waits do not consume this budget. Model calls overlapping local tools still count. An already admitted measurement may finish after model-budget exhaustion; new design tools close. Use client status for the latest collected model-time reading.

1: # Shared client
2: 
3: Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
4: Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
5: `atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
6: `atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
7: `atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
8: Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
9: The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
10: Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
11: Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
12: Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.
13:

read · measurement.md
Read file /workspace/measurement.md, lines 1-42
1: # LDO-LOW-VOLTAGE-SKY130 measurement contract
2: 
3: Numerical stimuli and limits come from spec.json. Connect the same submitted
4: DUT to VIN, VOUT, VSS and ideal noiseless VREF. The load is a prescribed ideal
5: finite-slew current sink; the external capacitor is ideal and bench supplied.
6: No startup, protection, bandgap, yield or layout checks are implied.
7: 
8: ## DC and load changes
9: 
10: Run DC at all three loads and light/heavy, middle/heavy and burst profiles.
11: Meter all DUT terminals. Input/reference currents enter DUT; output/ground
12: currents leave. Verify KCL within 1 nA. Overhead is
13: max(Iin-Iout,0)+max(Iref,0), including feedback and bias consumption.
14: Compare voltage against the fixed 1.0 V target, never the waveform tail.
15: Both isolated edge directions and the final falling burst edge have recovery
16: windows. Recovery is the final interpolated entry into the absolute required
17: band that remains there until the window ends. Retain overshoot, droop, initial
18: hold error, profile points and all signed currents.
19: 
20: Maximum sample gap is 5e-10 s. Every required window must be fully
21: covered with finite increasing data. Unsettled complete data retains null
22: recovery and an observation lower bound; it is MISS when that bound exceeds the
23: limit. Missing/truncated/invalid data is measurement failure. Unexecuted cases
24: are incomplete, never PASS. Inclusive limits are compared without rounding.
25: 
26: ## Supply measurements
27: 
28: Line stimulus starts at minimum VIN, rises to maximum, and returns to minimum,
29: at the group's highest load, process, temperature and capacitance. The starting
30: VIN label does not add a different line trajectory. PSRR saves native VIN/VOUT
31: phasors at 1 kHz and 1 MHz and reports 20 log10(abs(VIN/VOUT)). Check the native
32: regulated OP; zero/nonfinite transfers cannot produce infinite rejection.
33: 
34: Line and light/heavy-load PSRR are unscored reference measurements. Final
35: verification is 108 public, 18 independent and three characterization cases.
36: Each nominal group has six cases. No noise score is imposed on this variant.
37: 
38: ## Numerical precision
39: 
40: Independent evaluation uses the captured standard grid for every required case.
41: Numerical precision comparisons use a separately authorized developer allocation.
42: A standard-grid PASS is not a per-submission convergence proof.

1: # Netlist grammar
2: 
3: Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
4: Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
5: MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
6: Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
7: SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
8: ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
9: R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
10: Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.
11: Only used subcircuits are allowed. Validation checks each physical copy and each technology's geometry domain.
12: 
13: Per-polarity domains (micrometres), intersect all tt/ss/ff sections and W<=100, L>=0.15:
14: 
15: ```json

shell
ls -la /workspace; echo ---; wc -c /workspace/devices.md; echo ---; python3 -c "
import json,re
s=open('/workspace/devices.md').read()
m=re.search(r'\`\`\`json\n(.*?)\n\`\`\`',s,re.S)
d=json.loads(m.group(1))
for pol in d:
    print(pol, len(d[pol]))
    for sec,dom in d[pol].items():
        print('  ',sec,len(dom))
        # summarize: unique (wmin,wmax) -> l ranges
        from collections import defaultdict
        g=defaultdict(list)
        for e in dom: g[(e['wmin'],e['wmax'])].append((e['lmin'],e['lmax']))
        for k,v in g.items():
            print('     W',k,'L ranges:',sorted(v))
"

# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.
Only used subcircuits are allowed. Validation checks each physical copy and each technology's geometry domain.

3:Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.

/workspace/model.py
content
import math
# behavioral LDO transient model (pure python)
# nodes: VOUT, G (pass gate). Miller cap Cc between G and VOUT. Amp: gm_eff*(VFB-VREF) bounded by Imax
def sim(VIN=1.25, VLOAD_HI=1e-3, VLOAD_LO=25e-6, CL=200e-12, CC=12e-12,
        Wp=2000.0, Vth=0.45, Ispec=73e-9, n=1.35, Cg=0.7e-12,
        gm_eff=100e-6, Imax=35e-6, Rdiv=20e3, VREF=0.5, tstep=0.2e-9, tstop=2e-6,
        VOUT_init=1.0, verbose=False):
    UT = 0.02585
    nVT = n*UT
    def ipass(vsg):
        x = vsg - Vth
        a = math.exp(min(x/(2*nVT), 30.0))
        return Ispec*Wp*math.log(1+a)**2
    # find G for load current at init
    Iload = VLOAD_LO + 1.0*(VOUT_init)/Rdiv
    # solve ipass(VIN-G) = Iload
    lo, hi = 0.0, VIN
    for _ in range(200):
        mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
        if ipass(VIN-mid) > Iload: lo = mid
        else: hi = mid
    G = 0.5*(lo+hi)
    VOUT = VOUT_init
    t = 0.0
    vmax = VOUT; vmin = VOUT
    hist = []
    while t < tstop:
        # load profile: step up at 1us over 100ns
        if t < 1e-6: Iload = VLOAD_LO + VOUT/Rdiv
        elif t < 1e-6+100e-9: Iload = VLOAD_LO + (VLOAD_HI-VLOAD_LO)*(t-1e-6)/100e-9 + VOUT/Rdiv
        else: Iload = VLOAD_HI + VOUT/Rdiv
        VFB = VOUT/2.0
        err = VFB - VREF
        Iamp = gm_eff*err
        Iamp = max(-Imax, min(Imax, Iamp))
        Ip = ipass(VIN-G)
        dVOUT = (Ip - Iload)/CL
        # gate node: (Cg+Cc) dG/dt = Iamp + Cc*dVOUT/dt ; note Iamp sign: err>0 -> gate rises
        dG = (Iamp + CC*dVOUT)/(Cg+CC)
        VOUT += dVOUT*tstep
        G += dG*tstep
        t += tstep
        vmax = max(vmax,VOUT); vmin = min(vmin,VOUT)
        if verbose and abs(t-1.1e-6)<tstep/2: hist.append((t,VOUT,G))
    return dict(VOUT=VOUT, G=G, vmax=vmax, vmin=vmin, ipass_final=ipass(VIN-G))

if __name__ == '__main__':
    for cc in [0e-12, 2e-12, 5e-12, 12e-12, 20e-12]:
        r = sim(CC=cc)
        print("Cc=%5.1f pF  VOUT_final=%.4f  peak=%.4f (droop %5.0f mV)"%(cc*1e12, r['VOUT'], r['vmax'], (1.0-r['vmin'])*1e3))

import model as M
import math
# instrument: copy of sim with tracing
def sim2(CC=0.0, gm_eff=100e-6, Imax=35e-6, Wp=2000.0, Cg=0.7e-12, CL=200e-12, tstop=2e-6, tstep=0.5e-9, Rdiv=20e3, Vp=1.25):
    UT=0.02585; n=1.35; nVT=n*UT; Vth=0.45; Ispec=73e-9
    def ipass(vsg):
        a=math.exp(min((vsg-Vth)/(2*nVT),30.0)); return Ispec*Wp*math.log(1+a)**2
    Ilo=25e-6+1.0/Rdiv
    lo,hi=0.0,Vp
    for _ in range(100):
        mid=.5*(lo+hi)
        if ipass(Vp-mid)>Ilo: lo=mid
        else: hi=mid
    G=.5*(lo+hi); VOUT=1.0; t=0.0; vmin=1.0
    tr=[]
    while t<tstop:
        if t<1e-6: Il=25e-6+VOUT/Rdiv
        elif t<1.1e-6: Il=25e-6+(1e-3-25e-6)*(t-1e-6)/100e-9+VOUT/Rdiv
        else: Il=1e-3+VOUT/Rdiv
        err=VOUT/2-0.5
        Iamp=max(-Imax,min(Imax,gm_eff*err))
        Ip=ipass(Vp-G)
        dV=(Ip-Il)/CL
        dG=(Iamp+CC*dV)/(Cg+CC)
        VOUT+=dV*tstep; G+=dG*tstep; t+=tstep
        vmin=min(vmin,VOUT)
        if t<1.4e-6 and abs(t*1e9-round(t*1e9,1))<tstep/2 and (round(t*1e9)%5==0): tr.append((round(t*1e9,1),round(VOUT,4),round(G,4),round(Ip*1e6,1)))
    return vmin,tr

import math
def sim2(CC=0.0, gm_eff=100e-6, Imax=35e-6, Wp=2000.0, Cg=0.7e-12, CL=200e-12, tstop=3e-6, tstep=0.2e-9, Rdiv=20e3, Vp=1.25, hi=1e-3, lo_i=25e-6, Vth=0.45, Ispec=73e-9, trace=False):
    UT=0.02585; n=1.35; nVT=n*UT
    def ipass(vsg):
        a=math.exp(min((vsg-Vth)/(2*nVT),30.0)); return Ispec*Wp*math.log(1+a)**2
    Ilo=lo_i+1.0/Rdiv
    l,h=0.0,Vp
    for _ in range(100):
        mid=.5*(l+h)
        if ipass(Vp-mid)>Ilo: l=mid
        else: h=mid
    G=.5*(l+h); VOUT=1.0; t=0.0; vmin=1.0; vmax=1.0
    tr=[]
    while t<tstop:
        if t<1e-6: Il=lo_i+VOUT/Rdiv
        elif t<1.1e-6: Il=lo_i+(hi-lo_i)*(t-1e-6)/100e-9+VOUT/Rdiv
        else: Il=hi+VOUT/Rdiv
        err=VOUT/2-0.5
        Iamp=max(-Imax,min(Imax,gm_eff*err))
        Ip=ipass(Vp-G)
        dV=(Ip-Il)/CL
        dG=(Iamp+CC*dV)/(Cg+CC)
        VOUT+=dV*tstep; G+=dG*tstep; t+=tstep
        vmin=min(vmin,VOUT); vmax=max(vmax,VOUT)
        if trace and 1.0e-6<=t<=1.3e-6 and round(t*1e9)%10==0:
            tr.append((round(t*1e9),round(VOUT,4),round(G,4),round(Ip*1e6,1)))
    # recovery time to 30mV band after step
    return vmin,vmax,tr
for cc,args in [(0.0,dict(gm_eff=100e-6)),(0.0,dict(gm_eff=300e-6)),(5e-12,dict(gm_eff=100e-6)),(5e-12,dict(gm_eff=300e-6)),

    print("   ",tr)

import math
UT=0.02585
def run(CC=5e-12,gm=300e-6,Imax=60e-6,Wp=2000.,Cg=0.8e-12,CL=200e-12,Rdiv=20e3,

    nVT=n*UT
    def ipass(vsg):
        a=math.exp(min((vsg-Vth)/(2*nVT),30.0)); return Ispec*Wp*math.log(1+a)**2
    Ilo=25e-6+1.0/Rdiv
    l,h=0.0,VIN
    for _ in range(100):
        m=.5*(l+h)
        if ipass(VIN-m)>Ilo: l=m
        else: h=m
    G=.5*(l+h); VOUT=1.0; t=0.0; vmin=1.0; vmax=1.0; rec_dn=None; rec_up=None
    hist=[]
    while t<tstop:
        # profile: hold 1us, up-edge 1.0-1.1us, hold to 3us, down-edge 3.0-3.1us, hold
        if t<1e-6: Il=25e-6
        elif t<1.1e-6: Il=25e-6+(1e-3-25e-6)*(t-1e-6)/100e-9
        elif t<3e-6: Il=1e-3
        else: Il=25e-6
        Il+=VOUT/Rdiv+(VOUT)/ro_p
        err=VOUT/2-0.5
        Iamp=Imax*math.tanh(gm*err/Imax)
        Ip=ipass(VIN-G)
        dV=(Ip-Il)/CL
        dG=(Iamp+CC*dV)/(Cg+CC)
        VOUT+=dV*tstep; G+=dG*tstep; t+=tstep
        vmin=min(vmin,VOUT); vmax=max(vmax,VOUT)
        hist.append((t,VOUT))
    # recovery: last entry into +-30mV band after up-edge (1.0us) that stays
    def rec(t0,t1,target=0.03):
        bad=None
        for (tt,vv) in hist:
            if tt>t0 and abs(vv-1.0)>target: bad=tt
        return (bad-t0) if bad else 0.0
    return dict(droop=(1.0-vmin)*1e3, over=(vmax-1.0)*1e3, rec_up=rec(1.0e-6,8e-6), rec_dn=rec(3.0e-6,8e-6), vend=hist[-1][1])
if __name__=='__main__':
    best=[]
    for Cg in (0.5e-12,0.8e-12,1.5e-12,3e-12):
      for CC in (2e-12,5e-12,8e-12,12e-12):
        for gm in (100e-6,200e-6,300e-6,500e-6):
            r=run(CC=CC,gm=gm,Cg=Cg,Imax=100e-6)
            best.append((r['droop'],r['over'],r['rec_up'],r['rec_dn'],Cg,CC,gm,r['vend']))
    best.sort()
    for b in best[:14]:
        print("droop=%5.1f over=%5.1f rec_up=%7.3fus rec_dn=%7.3fus Cg=%4.1fp Cc=%4.1fp gm=%5.0fu vend=%.4f"%(
            b[0],b[1],b[2]*1e6,b[3]*1e6,b[4]*1e12,b[5]*1e12,b[6]*1e6,b[7]))
EOF

content
* LDO: PMOS pass, PMOS diff pair (current tail), NMOS mirror load, Miller comp
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
* --- pass device (source=VIN, gate=G, drain=VOUT) ---
XMP VOUT G VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=0.15 nf=1 m=3
* --- input pair: M1 gate=VFB (left, diode side), M2 gate=VREF (right -> output) ---
XM1 D1 VFB S VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=30 L=0.15 nf=1 m=1
XM2 G VREF S VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=30 L=0.15 nf=1 m=1
* --- tail PMOS current source ---
XMT S VB VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=50 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
* --- bias leg: diode PMOS + resistor ---
XMB VB VB VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=50 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
RB VB VSS 32k
* --- NMOS mirror load ---
XM3 D1 D1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=20 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
XM4 G D1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=20 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
* --- Miller compensation ---
CC G VOUT 5p
* --- feedback divider (1:1) ---
R1 VOUT VFB 25k
R2 VFB VSS 25k
.ends DUT

content
* LDO v3: PMOS pass, PMOS diff pair (current tail), NMOS mirror load, Miller comp
* pair L longer to suppress DIBL-induced load-dependent offset
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
* --- pass device (source=VIN, gate=G, drain=VOUT) ---
XMP VOUT G VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=0.15 nf=1 m=3
* --- input pair: M1 gate=VFB (left, diode side), M2 gate=VREF (right -> output) ---
XM1 D1 VFB S VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=50 L=0.25 nf=1 m=1
XM2 G VREF S VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=50 L=0.25 nf=1 m=1
* --- tail PMOS current source ---
XMT S VB VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=50 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
* --- bias leg: diode PMOS + resistor ---
XMB VB VB VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=50 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
RB VB VSS 32k
* --- NMOS mirror load ---
XM3 D1 D1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=20 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
XM4 G D1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=20 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
* --- Miller compensation ---
CC G VOUT 5p
* --- feedback divider (1:1) ---
R1 VOUT VFB 25k
R2 VFB VSS 25k
.ends DUT

write · dut.spice
/workspace/dut.spice
content
* LDO v4: pmos pass driven via diode level shift + sink (current-mirror style gate drive)
* main OTA (pmos pair, current tail, nmos mirror) + local fast degenerated pair, both into sink gate
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
* --- pass device; diode sets gate level (mirror ratio ~22) ---
XMP VOUT G VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=0.15 nf=1 m=3
XMD G G VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=45 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
* --- sink from gate to vss, gate=NK ---
XMS G NK VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=20 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
* --- main OTA: pmos pair, tail, nmos mirror; output NK ---
XM1 D1 VFB S VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=50 L=0.25 nf=1 m=1
XM2 NK VREF S VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=50 L=0.25 nf=1 m=1
XMT S VB VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=50 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
XMB VB VB VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=50 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
RB VB VSS 16k
XM3 D1 D1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=20 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
XM4 NK D1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=20 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
* --- local fast amp: degenerated pmos pair + own mirror; output NK ---
XM5 D2 VFB S5 VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=20 L=0.15 nf=1 m=1
XM6 NK VREF S6 VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=20 L=0.15 nf=1 m=1
RRL S5 VIN 100k
RRM S6 VIN 100k
XM7 D2 D2 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=20 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
XM8 NK D2 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=20 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
* --- compensation ---
CC NK VOUT 15p
* --- feedback divider (1:1) ---
R1 VOUT VFB 10k
R2 VFB VSS 10k
.ends DUT

write · dut.spice
/workspace/dut.spice
content
* LDO final: PMOS pass (direct gate drive), PMOS diff pair with current tail,
* NMOS mirror load, Miller compensation, 1:1 resistor feedback divider.
* Pair uses L=0.25 to suppress DIBL-induced load dependent offset.
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
* --- pass device (source=VIN, gate=G, drain=VOUT) ---
XMP VOUT G VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=0.15 nf=1 m=3
* --- input pair: M1 gate=VFB (left, mirror side), M2 gate=VREF (right -> gate node) ---
XM1 D1 VFB S VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=50 L=0.25 nf=1 m=1
XM2 G VREF S VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=50 L=0.25 nf=1 m=1
* --- tail PMOS current source (mirror of bias leg) ---
XMT S VB VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=50 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
XMB VB VB VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=50 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
RB VB VSS 16k
* --- NMOS mirror load ---
XM3 D1 D1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=20 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
XM4 G D1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=20 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
* --- Miller compensation ---
CC G VOUT 12p
* --- feedback divider (1:1, low impedance for fast loop and low gate swing) ---
R1 VOUT VFB 7.5k
R2 VFB VSS 7.5k
.ends DUT

* low-impedance 1:1 feedback divider.  Pair L=0.25 suppresses DIBL load-dependent offset.
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
* --- pass device (source=VIN, gate=G, drain=VOUT) ---
XMP VOUT G VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=0.15 nf=1 m=3
* --- input pair: M1 gate=VFB (left, mirror side), M2 gate=VREF (right -> gate node) ---
XM1 D1 VFB S VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=50 L=0.25 nf=1 m=1
XM2 G VREF S VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=50 L=0.25 nf=1 m=1
* --- tail PMOS current source (mirror of bias leg) ---
XMT S VB VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=50 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
XMB VB VB VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=50 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
RB VB VSS 12k
* --- NMOS mirror load ---
XM3 D1 D1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=20 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
XM4 G D1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=20 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1
* --- compensation: Miller cap with series nulling resistor ---
CC G NC1 20p
RC NC1 VOUT 10k
* --- feedback divider (1:1, low impedance: fast loop, small gate swing) ---
R1 VOUT VFB 7.5k
R2 VFB VSS 7.5k
.ends DUT

cd /workspace && atb-client submit --rationale "Topology: PMOS pass device (W=100um,L=0.15um,m=3) driven directly from a 5T error amplifier; PMOS input pair (VFB on mirror-side device, VREF on output-side device) at 0.5V input CM, biased by a current-source tail mirrored from a resistor-referenced PMOS bias leg (RB=12k); NMOS mirror load; series-RC/null-Rz Miller compensation (20pF + 10k) from pass gate to VOUT; 1:1 low-impedance resistor feedback divider (2x7.5k) generating VFB=VOUT/2. Key design choices driven by measured iteration: (1) pair L=0.25um (not 0.15) to suppress the DIBL-induced load-dependent offset that made the DC error -58mV at the heavy load; (2) low-impedance 15k total divider both for a fast, well-defined loop pole and to raise the light-load pass current, reducing the pass-device Vgs swing between light and heavy load; (3) Rz=10k in series with the 20pF Miller cap (tuned empirically over 5 variants) for damping with usable transient; (4) total quiescent 90uA at light load, under the 100uA overhead limit at nominal VIN. Measured at N: DC error +0.2/-8.1/-17.3 mV (PASS all three), overhead 90uA (PASS), VREF current 0 (PASS), initial-hold error <=8.1mV (PASS), all recovery times 1-3us (PASS, limit 5us), mid-heavy envelope error 78.6mV (PASS, limit 80mV). Residual miss: light-heavy and burst envelope error 129mV vs 80mV limit; the required pass-gate swing for the 25uA->1mA step (~86mV, weak-inversion Vgs range over the 11.8x current ratio) plus the finite gain of the Miller feedforward can only be reduced by adding local gain in the gate drive path; mirror-drive (diode+sink) and extra fast-auxiliary-amplifier variants were built and measured but could not be stabilized within the remaining tuning budget (they latched/oscillated), so the stable, DC-accurate variant with all recoveries passing was submitted." 2>&1 | head -30

MODEL
0:39:25

Submission accepted and frozen (dut-199f35d16674f5143de5b189). Session complete.

Design summary (independent participant):

Circuit — PMOS pass device driven directly from a 5-transistor error amplifier: PMOS differential pair (VFB/VREF, 0.5 V CM) with a current-source tail mirrored from a resistor-referenced PMOS bias leg, NMOS mirror load, series-RC (nulling-resistor) Miller compensation to VOUT, and a low-impedance 1:1 resistive feedback divider (VFB = VOUT/2).

Major decisions (all driven by client measurements):

  • Pair L = 0.25 µm, not minimum: the 0.15 µm pair showed a strong DIBL-induced, load-dependent offset that produced −58 mV DC error at heavy load; lengthening the pair cut it to −17 mV.
  • Low-impedance divider (2×7.5 kΩ, 67 µA): pushes the loop's load pole up and pre-loads the pass device so its Vgs swing over the load range shrinks.
  • Miller cap with 10 kΩ nulling resistor (tuned across 5 measured variants): best damping/transient trade-off; plain Miller caps gave 146 mV excursions, unbuffered/50–100 kΩ Rz variants oscillated.
  • Bias budget: 90 µA total quiescent at light load (limit 100 µA).

Measured results at N (nominal): DC error +0.2/−8.1/−17.3 mV (all PASS), overhead 90 µA (PASS), VREF current 0 (PASS), initial-hold error ≤ 8 mV (PASS), all recovery times 1–3 µs (PASS, limit 5 µs), mid-heavy envelope 78.6 mV (PASS). Residual miss: light-heavy/burst envelope 129 mV vs 80 mV limit — the 25 µA→1 mA step needs ~86 mV of pass-gate swing, which the direct-drive loop's feedforward gain cannot beat; mirror-drive (diode+sink) and auxiliary fast-amplifier variants were built and measured but could not be stabilized in the remaining budget.
